## Classification

- `static-primary`
- `official-syllabus`
- `official-pyq`
- `official-key`
- `judgment-or-law`
- `book-or-academic`
- `current-primary`
- `reliable-secondary`
- `inference`

An inference must identify the verified facts from which it is derived. It cannot be used as a
substitute for a missing factual source.
